                          - heading "How Do You Get Perma-Evade?" [level=4] [ref=e1642]:
                            - strong [ref=e1643]: How Do You Get Perma-Evade?
                          - paragraph [ref=e1644]:
                            - text: On this build
                            - strong [ref=e1645]: Perma-Evade
                            - strong [ref=e1646]
                            - text: is not longer truely achievable but we do our best to bridge the gap with the mobility options we have available to us.
                          - paragraph [ref=e1647]:
                            - text: We now use
                            - generic [ref=e1652]: Evolving Claws Rushing Claw
                            - text: which had the tech moved into it.
                          - paragraph [ref=e1653]:
                            - text: This allows us to to
                            - strong [ref=e1654]: Perma
                            - generic [ref=e1659]: Rushing Claw
                            - text: via
                            - strong [ref=e1660]: Locking On
                            - text: to
                            - strong [ref=e1661]: Enemies
                            - text: but it will now require
                            - strong [ref=e1662]: 30 Ranks
                            - text: in
                            - generic [ref=e1667]: Ravager
                            - text: to achieve as the
                            - strong [ref=e1668]: +1 Minimum Ferocity
                            - text: was removed as an upgrade option on
                            - generic [ref=e1673]: Ravager
                            - text: .

                            - strong [ref=e1677]: Attack Speed
